./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:3:- 2026-06-12: AMB-674 / PLOS-046 defines Source Atlas freshness cadence as a calm, explicit, safety-subordinate control policy. Revocation, signer trust, compatibility, rollback, release receipt, source authority, jurisdiction/high-risk policy, and privacy-boundary checks outrank cadence; stale public Source Atlas material must be disclosed as stale/degraded/source-needed/review-needed/blocked instead of silently claimed current.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:4:- 2026-06-12: AMB-673 / PLOS-045 defines the future app remote Source Atlas flow as manifest-first and fail-closed. Safety outranks freshness: no fresher remote pack can become runtime-eligible until manifest, compatibility, freshness, revocation, rollback, release receipt, source authority, and exact pack verification gates pass; failures quarantine and route to verified local fallback, source-needed, degraded, or blocked behavior.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:5:- 2026-06-12: AMB-672 / PLOS-044 defines release rings and rollback manifests as first-class Source Atlas control metadata. Production promotion is Red without rollback path/drill ownership, and rollback must preserve bad/target provenance, release receipts, signer/trust state, manifest ids, reason, severity, effective date, and owner-visible operation receipt instead of overwriting, deleting, or relabeling artifacts.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:7:- 2026-06-12: AMB-670 / PLOS-042 defines signed manifests and compatibility manifests as fail-closed public Source Atlas control objects. Unsigned, incompatible, unsupported, stale, revoked, hash-mismatched, missing-release-receipt, missing-rollback, missing-revocation, unknown-signer, or private-data-containing manifests cannot select production runtime-eligible packs and must route to quarantine/fallback.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:8:- 2026-06-12: AMB-669 / PLOS-041 defines released public Source Atlas pack paths as immutable content-bound addresses. Version names express release intent, but runtime/tooling must require exact hash, signed manifest/index pointer, release receipt, revocation, rollback, freshness, compatibility, and source authority before eligibility. Supersession publishes new pointer/manifests and new immutable paths instead of overwriting released pack bytes.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:9:- 2026-06-12: AMB-668 / PLOS-040 defines the R2 layout as immutable public Source Atlas distribution, not personal storage. Production object keys must be content-addressed and ring-scoped; current pointers live in signed manifests/indexes; allowed families are packs, seeds, manifests, freshness, revocations, compatibility, validation reports, release receipts, and rollback manifests. User goals, captures, schedules, proof, receipts, replay, local learning, diagnostics, exports, private imports, identifiers, raw private text, secrets, and write tokens are structurally forbidden from keys, bodies, metadata, requests, logs, and support artifacts.
